# Regression-channel reversion: frozen Hyperliquid study (#1644)

Overall verdict: **FAIL** (proxy inconclusive, venue fail).

- Proxy M1 conclusion: **inconclusive**. held-out evidence is incomplete: 2023 atr_band_revert: 6 incomplete funded legs; 2023 atr_band_revert: 6 incumbent datasets with incomplete funding; 2023 candidate: 6 incomplete funded legs; 2023 candidate: 6 incumbent datasets with incomplete funding; 2023 mean_reversion_pro: 6 incomplete funded legs; 2023 mean_reversion_pro: 6 incumbent datasets with incomplete funding.
- Venue-input conclusion: **fail**. failed frozen checks: beats_mean_reversion_pro_sharpe_x1, net_positive_x1, net_positive_x2.

Registry evidence source for this outcome: `study_fail`. edge_status no_edge in every outcome: hidden from discovery, explicit paper mode runs it, live use still needs allow_no_edge: true (the repository's acknowledged-live exception); a research pass approves nothing and promotion is a separate reviewed action.

Generated by `run_study.py` from `results.json`; do not edit by hand. Proxy candles are not evidence of a Hyperliquid edge.

## Frozen inputs

- Protocol sha256 `e3aab415aa0d6a432ff215f2128ae2e15af25cf3880f53fb8113645100892b88` (printed by `--protocol-hash` before scoring)
- Proxy freeze `backtest/candidates/regression_channel_reversion_1644/proxy_freeze.json` sha256 `0e5b319a6710f3c8842072af2600fb5f4bdce8f569b24e44fcd1311233a0c44c`; 30 frame digests and funding digests for BTC, ETH, SOL verified before scoring; network fetches were blocked while scoring.
- Venue manifest `backtest/candidates/regression_channel_reversion_1644_manifest.json` sha256 `c9348eab777868a96c8df8ca43d916d7931167fe31eadbf687e312d23bb99440` (venue: Hyperliquid mainnet perpetual candles from the public info API (candleSnapshot); closed bars only)
- Contract for every arm and every M1 incumbent: direction `both`, one `tiered_tp_pct` close (profit 0.03, close fraction 1.0), one fixed entry ATR x1.0 stop, comparison mode `strict`, capital $1000, intrabar `ohlc_walk`. Entries decided on bar N fill at the bar N+1 open.
- Comparators: `atr_band_revert` {"allow_short": true, "atr_period": 14, "k_entry": 1.5, "period": 20}, `mean_reversion_pro` {"adx_max": 25.0, "adx_period": 14, "confirm_window": 3, "entry_std": 2.0, "lookback": 30, "rsi_overbought": 70.0, "rsi_oversold": 30.0, "rsi_period": 14, "touch_entry": 0, "turn_entry": 0}
- Selection rule: candidate only, direction both, the seed close and stop, scored on the selection window (proxy: M1 `is`; venue: manifest `train`): eligible = every dataset scored, no incomplete funded leg, no liquidated leg, venue candle and funding coverage complete, >= 10 opening events (positions); pick max mean Sharpe, then max mean drawdown-adjusted return, then earliest optimizer-grid order; no eligible combination = inconclusive.
- Proxy verdict rule: inconclusive when any held-out window (oos, 2023, 2024, 2025H1) has a missing dataset, an incomplete funded leg (candidate, comparator or M1 incumbent), a funding row fetched during scoring or a frame whose hash differs from the freeze; when no `is` combination is eligible; or when the candidate has fewer than 30 independent position clusters in oos. Otherwise pass only if the contract-aware M1 verdict is pass in oos and in at least 2 of 2023, 2024 and 2025H1, the candidate oos mean return is positive, and the candidate oos mean Sharpe beats mean_reversion_pro and atr_band_revert under the same contract; else fail. The `is` window (2025-06-10 to 2026-01-01) overlaps 2025H1 for 2025-06-10 to 2025-07-01, so 2025H1 is not independent of selection over that span.
- Venue verdict rule: inconclusive when venue candle or funding coverage is incomplete, any funded leg is incomplete, the provenance is not venue data, no `train` combination is eligible, or the candidate has fewer than 30 independent position clusters in `test`. Otherwise pass only if the candidate mean return is positive at cost x1 and x2, the contract-aware M1 verdict on `test` at cost x1 is pass, and the candidate mean Sharpe beats mean_reversion_pro and atr_band_revert at cost x1; else fail.
- Overall rule: pass only if the proxy and venue conclusions both pass; fail if either is fail; otherwise inconclusive. The registry keeps edge_status no_edge in every outcome.

- Scoring span: cached M1 legs load only the window's own bars, so every arm (candidate, comparators and incumbents) warms up inside the window: the candidate's first decision is row fit_window + 1 of each window (row 51 at the seed, row 198 at fit_window 197).
- Proxy limitations: Binance US USDT candles stand in for Hyperliquid prices. Proxy legs use the flat M1 cost model (Hyperliquid taker fee plus the engine's default slippage); they model no lot rounding, no minimum order and no spread. The smallest simulated proxy order is compared with the pinned venue gate above. Smallest simulated proxy order $141.59 against the venue gate $10.30 (below gate: no).
- Registry defaults in oos (reference only): mean ret -25.54%, Sharpe -1.201, positions 1013, M1 fail.
- Parameter stability: Spearman train vs oos Sharpe 0.394; oos net-positive variants 5/420; plateau {"neighbor_count": 7, "neighbor_median_sharpe": -0.1608, "neighbor_min_sharpe": -0.3373, "neighbor_positive_sharpe": 2}.

## Venue-input study (frozen Hyperliquid candles and funding, execution specification)

- Costs: taker 0.045%, maker 0.015%, slippage 5.0 bps per side plus per-coin half spread, lot rounding to szDecimals, minimum order $10 with a 3% margin, funding booked hourly. Identical for candidate, comparators and M1 incumbents.
- Window `train` [2024-09-01, 2025-09-01): selection: parameter sweeps and the frozen selection rule read only this window; previously used by the #1643, #1645, #1646, #1647, #1649, #1656, #1660 and #1666 studies
- Window `test` [2025-09-01, 2026-09-01): held-out: scored once with the frozen selections; previously used as the held-out window of the same earlier studies, so this is retrospective candidate evidence
- `BTC 4h`: szDecimals 5, half spread 0.06 bps, candles `89f84288d462b830`, funding `b961391682a15a61`
- `ETH 4h`: szDecimals 4, half spread 0.19 bps, candles `a17523ecbb30f7b5`, funding `1b541abb8801a444`
- `SOL 4h`: szDecimals 2, half spread 0.42 bps, candles `5ca4fa74940f38f1`, funding `a498781bca4cf017`
- Scoring span: manifest warm-up of 300 bars precedes each window, which covers the largest fit_window + 2 = 199 rows, so every held-out bar is decidable.
- Limitations: The venue windows cover 2024-09-01 to 2026-09-01 only (the venue serves the most recent 5,000 candles); the M1 2023 and 2024 windows have no venue equivalent. Spread is a per-coin half spread from one frozen L2 snapshot plus flat slippage; queue position, partial fills, liquidation and funding-rate impact are not modelled.

Selection on `train`: rule outcome `selected`, 324 of 420 combinations eligible; selected `{"band_multiplier": 1.5, "fit_window": 100, "min_residual_std": 0.0, "slope_ceiling": 0.05}`.

## Decision parity

Grid decision parity: for every grid and boundary parameter set on every frozen venue frame (held-out window plus manifest warm-up): every entry bar, every stride-th bar from the first decidable bar and the last bar are re-evaluated from the historical prefix ending there and, once decidable, from the 200-row and 199-row bounded tails ending there; every listed column must equal the full-series value. 423 parameter sets x 3 frames, 574098 re-evaluations, 0 mismatches; 981 of 1269 frame/parameter rows emit both long and short entries. ## Verdict guards

The same rules on altered copies of this result: proxy_below_sample_floor -> inconclusive, proxy_funding_incomplete -> inconclusive, proxy_missing_dataset -> inconclusive, proxy_no_selection -> inconclusive, venue_below_sample_floor -> inconclusive, venue_coverage_incomplete -> inconclusive, venue_funding_incomplete -> inconclusive, venue_no_selection -> inconclusive, venue_proxy_provenance -> inconclusive.
